    <script type="rocketlazyloadscript">
        let popupStartTime = null;

        function getPopupSeenDate() {
            const stored = localStorage.getItem("popupSeen");
            return stored ? new Date(stored) : null;
        }

        function shouldShowPopup() {
            const lastSeen = getPopupSeenDate();
            if (!lastSeen) return true;
            const now = new Date();
            const diffInDays = Math.floor((now - lastSeen) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
            return diffInDays >= 30;
        }

        function showPopupIfNeeded() {
            if (shouldShowPopup()) {
                const popup = document.getElementById("welcomePopup");
                if (popup) {
                    popup.classList.add("visible");
                    showStep('popup-step-1');
                    popupStartTime = new Date();

                    if (typeof gtag === 'function') {
                        gtag('event', 'popup_viewed', {
                            'event_category': 'Popup',
                            'event_label': 'shown'
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        function showStep(id) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.popup-step').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
            const step = document.getElementById(id);
            if (step) step.classList.add('active');
        }

        function handleUserChoice(type) {
            localStorage.setItem("popupSeen", new Date().toISOString());
            localStorage.setItem("popupUserType", type);

            if (typeof gtag === 'function') {
                gtag('event', 'popup_choice', {
                    'event_category': 'Popup',
                    'event_label': type
                });
            }

            if (type === 'buyer') {
                showStep('popup-step-buyer');
            } else {
                setTimeout(() => {
                    const url = (type === 'future') ? "https://secondhandy.com.pl/newsletter/" : "https://bit.ly/sh_connect";
                    window.location.href = url;
                }, 150);
            }
        }

        function trackAndRedirect(label, url) {
            trackPopupDuration();
            if (typeof gtag === 'function') {
                gtag('event', 'popup_action', {
                    'event_category': 'Popup',
                    'event_label': label
                });
            }
            window.location.href = url;
        }

        function trackAndClose(label) {
            trackPopupDuration();
            if (typeof gtag === 'function') {
                gtag('event', 'popup_action', {
                    'event_category': 'Popup',
                    'event_label': label
                });
            }
            const popup = document.getElementById("welcomePopup");
            popup.classList.remove("visible");
            setTimeout(() => popup.style.display = "none", 400);
        }

        function trackPopupDuration() {
            if (popupStartTime && typeof gtag === 'function') {
                const now = new Date();
                const seconds = Math.round((now - popupStartTime) / 1000);
                gtag('event', 'popup_duration', {
                    'event_category': 'Popup',
                    'value': seconds
                });
            }
        }

        function closePopup() {
            localStorage.setItem("popupSeen", new Date().toISOString());
            trackPopupDuration();
            if (typeof gtag === 'function') {
                gtag('event', 'popup_close', {
                    'event_category': 'Popup',
                    'event_label': 'zamknieto_X'
                });
            }
            const popup = document.getElementById("welcomePopup");
            popup.classList.remove("visible");
            setTimeout(() => popup.style.display = "none", 400);
        }

        function showGroups() {
            if (typeof gtag === 'function') {
                gtag('event', 'popup_action', {
                    'event_category': 'Popup',
                    'event_label': 'dolacz_do_grupy_fb'
                });
            }
            showStep('popup-step-groups');
        }

        window.addEventListener("load", showPopupIfNeeded);
    </script>

function headersearchfade() {

var scroll = jQuery(window).scrollTop();
    if (scroll > 330) { 
        jQuery("#c27-header-search-form").css("opacity",1); 
        jQuery("#c27-header-search-form").find("input").show();
    }
    else { 
        jQuery("#c27-header-search-form").css("opacity", 0); 
        jQuery("#c27-header-search-form").find("input").blur();
        jQuery("#c27-header-search-form").find("input").hide();
    }

}

}

(function($) {
	
  	/* Clicking package boxes */
    clickPackageBox();

	/* Event Calendar */ 
    googleCalendarScript();

    function clickPackageBox() {
        // Page id, will only work with this page
        if ( ! $('.page-id-127').length ) {
            return false;
        }
        $('.job_listing_packages .pricing-item.c27-pick-package.cts-pricing-item').on('click', function() {
            let elemButton = $(this).find('a.select-plan')
            if ( elemButton.length ) {
                elemButton.trigger('click');
            }
        });
    }

    function googleCalendarScript() {
        if(!$(".add-to-google-cal").length && $(".block-field-job_description").length) {
            return false;
        } 

        var blockText = $.trim($(".block-field-job_description .pf-body").html()),
        sourceHref = window.location.href; 
        $(".add-to-google-cal").each(function(i,e){
            if (blockText != '') {
                var oldHref = $(e).attr('href'),
                // refText = stripQueryStringAndHashFromPath(sourceHref);
                // refText += '<br>';
                // refText += '<br>';
                refText = blockText;

                var newHref = replaceUrlParam(oldHref, 'details', refText);
                $(e).attr('href', newHref); 
            }
        });

        function replaceUrlParam(url, paramName, paramValue)
        {
            if (paramValue == null) {
                paramValue = '';
            }
            var pattern = new RegExp('\\b('+paramName+'=).*?(&|#|$)');
            if (url.search(pattern)>=0) {
                return url.replace(pattern,'$1' + paramValue + '$2');
            }
            url = url.replace(/[?#]$/,'');
            return url + (url.indexOf('?')>0 ? '&' : '?') + paramName + '=' + paramValue;
        }

        function stripQueryStringAndHashFromPath(url)
        {
          return url.split("?")[0].split("#")[0];
        }
    }

})(jQuery);

function initCountDownMod() {
  if( jQuery('.countdown-box.countdown-block').length ) {
      let addCountInt = 0;
      jQuery('.countdown-box.countdown-block .countdown-list li').each( function(){
          let countText = jQuery(this).find('p').text();
          addCountInt += parseInt( countText );
      });
      if ( ! addCountInt ) {
        let useText = 'WYDARZENIE ODBYWA SIĘ DZIŚ!';
        if ( ! jQuery('body').hasClass('type-event')) {    
            useText = 'Dostawa dziś!';
        }
          jQuery('.countdown-box.countdown-block .pf-body ul').html('<li style="width: 100%;"><p style="padding:0; width: 0;"></p><span style="font-size: 21px; font-weight: 600; top: 12px;">'+ useText +'</span></li>');
      }
  }
}

});
function viewFinderIcon() {
    if (jQuery('#explore-map-location-ctrl').length && jQuery('.mapboxgl-canvas').length) {
      jQuery('#explore-map-location-ctrl').on('click', function() {
          if (!jQuery(this).hasClass("codicts-marker-active")) {
              jQuery(this).addClass("codicts-marker-active");
          } else {
              jQuery(this).removeClass("codicts-marker-active");
          }
      });

      let isMouseDown = false;
      jQuery('.mapboxgl-canvas').on('mousedown', function() {
          isMouseDown = true;
      }).on('mouseup', function() {
          isMouseDown = false;
      }).on('mousemove', function() {
          if (isMouseDown && jQuery('#explore-map-location-ctrl').hasClass("codicts-marker-active")) {
              jQuery('#explore-map-location-ctrl').removeClass("codicts-marker-active");
          }
      }); 
      
      var urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      if ( urlParams ) {
        if( urlParams.has('search_location') ) { 
          MyListing.Geocoder.getUserLocation({
              receivedAddress: function (e) {            
                if ( urlParams.get('search_location') == e.address )
                { 
                  jQuery('.location-filter > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
                  jQuery('.ehs-location > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
                }
              }
          });
        }
      }
      
    }

    let timeouthandlr;

    jQuery('body').on('click', '.location-filter > i', function() {
        let inputFeild = jQuery(this).parent().find('input').first();
        let elem = jQuery(this);
        if ( timeouthandlr ) {
            clearTimeout( timeouthandlr );
        }
        if (!elem.hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
            elem.addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
            jQuery('.ehs-location > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
        } else {
            timeouthandlr = setTimeout( function(){
                inputFeild.val("");
                inputFeild[0].dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
                elem.removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
                if (jQuery('.ehs-location input').length) {
                    jQuery('.ehs-location > i').removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
                    jQuery('.ehs-location input').val('');
                }
            },300);
        }
    });

    jQuery('body').on('click', '.ehs-location > i', function() {
        let inputFeild = jQuery(this).parent().find('input').first();
        let elem = jQuery(this);
        if ( timeouthandlr ) {
            clearTimeout( timeouthandlr );
        }
        if (!elem.hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
            elem.addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
            jQuery('.location-filter > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
        } else {
            // if (jQuery('.location-filter input').length) {
            //     jQuery('.location-filter input').first().val('');
            //     jQuery('.location-filter input')[0].dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('input'));
            // }
            timeouthandlr = setTimeout( function(){
                inputFeild.val("");
                inputFeild[0].dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
                elem.removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
                jQuery('.location-filter > i').removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
            },300);
        }
    });
	
	  let curVal = ''; 
    jQuery('.location-filter > input').on("keydown", function() {
      		
       // if (jQuery(this).val() == "") {
            if (jQuery('.location-filter > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
                jQuery('.location-filter > i').removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
				curVal = jQuery(this).val();      
            }
            if (jQuery('.ehs-location > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
                jQuery('.ehs-location > i').removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
            }
      //  }
    }).on("keyup",function(){
    	let newVal = jQuery(this).val();   
        if(newVal != "" && newVal == curVal) {
          jQuery('.location-filter > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
             jQuery('.ehs-location > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
        }
    });

    jQuery('body').on("keydown", '.ehs-location > input', function() {
        if (jQuery(this).val() == "") {
            if (jQuery('.location-filter > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
                jQuery('.location-filter > i').removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
            }
            if (jQuery('.ehs-location > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
                jQuery('.ehs-location > i').removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
            }
        }
    });

    jQuery('.reset-results-27').on("click", function() {
        if (jQuery('.location-filter > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
            jQuery('.location-filter > i').removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
        }
        if (jQuery('.ehs-location > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
            jQuery('.ehs-location > i').removeClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
        }
    });

    if (jQuery('.orderby-filter select').val() == 'najblizej-mnie') {
        if (!jQuery('.location-filter > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
            jQuery('.location-filter > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
        }
        if (!jQuery('.ehs-location > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
            jQuery('.ehs-location > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
        }
    }

  	if ( jQuery('.orderby-filter select').length ) {
      let selectInterval;
      selectInterval = setInterval(function() {
          if (jQuery('.orderby-filter select.select2-hidden-accessible').length) {
              jQuery('.orderby-filter select').on('select2:select', function() {
                  if (jQuery(this).val() == 'najblizej-mnie') {
                      if (!jQuery('.location-filter > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
                          jQuery('.location-filter > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
                      }
                      if (!jQuery('.ehs-location > i').hasClass("codicts-marker-active-i")) {
                          jQuery('.ehs-location > i').addClass("codicts-marker-active-i");
                      }
                  }
              });
              clearInterval(selectInterval);
          }
      }, 500);
    }

}
